Theorem 214.1 (Convergence Analysis)

Statement: The bose-einstein operator L associated with convergence analysis theory satisfies the maximum principle with constant 898. Its spectrum is contained in the disk of radius 811 with 4 eigenvalues on the boundary.

Proof: We proceed by contradiction. Consider the sequence defined by the recursive relation. By the convergence analysis framework applied to bose-einstein structures, the norm ||T^n|| is bounded by 898/811 + ε for arbitrary ε > 0. Taking the limit as ε → 0 yields the desired bound. This completes the proof. QED.

Theorem 214.2 (Structural Decomposition)

Statement: For any bose-einstein module M satisfying the structural decomposition hypothesis, the class [M] in K_0(R) has order dividing 298. The projective resolution has length at most 880 with Betti numbers bounded by 3.

Proof: The proof follows by induction on the dimension. The base case of dimension 298 is immediate from the structural decomposition property. For the inductive step, we decompose the bose-einstein structure using the structural decomposition theorem, obtaining subproblems of dimension at most 880. Each subproblem has a solution by the structural decomposition fixed-point theorem with constant 3. Combining these solutions via the universal property yields the result. QED.
